Implement the following plan:

# Phase 3b: Ordinal Scale Normalization with Agent-Assisted Concept Resolution

## Context

Phase 3 unit/value normalization is complete (157 tests). But ordinal scoring systems (ECOG, Karnofsky, NYHA, Child-Pugh, GCS, APACHE, etc.) get `unit_concept_id = None` and `value_concept_id = None` because the normalizer is context-free and skips numeric values.

## Design Philosophy: Lookup → Agent → Approve → Persist

Instead of hardcoding all OMOP concept IDs upfront (which requires Athena verification), the system uses a three-tier resolution pattern:

1. **Lookup**: Check YAML config for a previously-approved mapping → use it immediately
2. **Agent**: If not found, an LLM agent proposes a mapping (SNOMED code → OMOP concept ID resolution)
3. **Approve & Persist**: Human reviews the proposal, approves/modifies it, and it gets written back to the YAML for all future lookups

This means the YAML starts with **structural knowledge** (entity aliases, LOINC codes, SNOMED codes — all verifiable from public sources) but OMOP concept IDs are populated incrementally through the agent/approval workflow.

## Research Summary

- `{score}` (OMOP 8527) is the standard UCUM unit for ordinal scales ([LOINC 89247-1](https://loinc.org/89247-1) uses `{score}` as its example unit)
- ECOG: [LOINC 89247-1](https://loinc.org/89247-1), answer list [LL529-9](https://loinc.org/LL529-9), answer codes LA9622-7 through LA9627-6
- SNOMED CT ECOG grades: 425389002 (grade 0) through 423409001 (grade 5) — verified via [BioPortal](https://bioportal.bioontology.org/ontologies/SNOMEDCT?p=classes&conceptid=424122007)
- No new DB column needed — `unit_concept_id` + `value_concept_id` + `entity_concept_id` fully describe ordinal measurements per [OMOP CDM v5.4](https://ohdsi.github.io/CommonDataModel/cdm54.html)
- Positive/negative results keep `unit_concept_id = NULL` per OMOP standard

## Files to Modify

| File | Change |
|------|--------|
| `config/ucum_mappings.yaml` | Add `{score}` unit + `ordinal_scales` section (structural + SNOMED, no hardcoded OMOP IDs initially) |
| `tools/unit_normalizer.py` | Add `_load_ordinal_scales()`, `normalize_ordinal_value()`, `propose_ordinal_mappings()` |
| `tools/structure_builder.py` | Update import + ordinal-first dispatch in `_create_atomic_from_mapping()` |
| `tests/test_unit_normalizer.py` | Add `TestNormalizeOrdinalValue` class (~18 tests) |
| `tests/test_phase3_integration.py` | Update ECOG expected values + add ordinal criteria tests |

## Step 1: YAML Structure

Add `{score}` to the existing `units:` list:
```yaml
  - canonical: "{score}"
    omop_unit_concept_id: 8527
    aliases: ["score", "points"]
```

Add `ordinal_scales:` top-level section. Note: `omop_value_concept_id` is **optional** per value — starts `null` until resolved by the agent/approval workflow. The SNOMED codes and LOINC answer codes are factual and present from the start.

```yaml
ordinal_scales:
  ecog:
    entity_aliases:
      - "ECOG"
      - "ECOG performance status"
      - "ECOG PS"
      - "Eastern Cooperative Oncology Group"
      - "WHO performance status"
      - "Zubrod score"
    loinc_code: "89247-1"
    loinc_answer_list: "LL529-9"
    unit_concept_id: 8527  # {score} — verified OMOP standard
    values:
      "0": { snomed_code: "425389002", loinc_answer: "LA9622-7", description: "Fully active" }
      "1": { snomed_code: "422512005", loinc_answer: "LA9623-5", description: "Restricted" }
      "2": { snomed_code: "422894000", loinc_answer: "LA9624-3", description: "Ambulatory, no work" }
      "3": { snomed_code: "423053003", loinc_answer: "LA9625-0", description: "Limited selfcare" }
      "4": { snomed_code: "423237006", loinc_answer: "LA9626-8", description: "Completely disabled" }
      "5": { snomed_code: "423409001", loinc_answer: "LA9627-6", description: "Dead" }

  karnofsky:
    entity_aliases:
      - "Karnofsky"
      - "KPS"
      - "Karnofsky performance status"
      - "Karnofsky performance scale"
    loinc_code: "89243-0"
    loinc_answer_list: "LL4986-7"
    unit_concept_id: 8527
    values:
      "100": { description: "Normal, no complaints" }
      "90":  { description: "Able to carry on normal activity" }
      "80":  { description: "Normal activity with effort" }
      "70":  { description: "Cares for self, unable to do active work" }
      "60":  { description: "Requires occasional assistance" }
      "50":  { description: "Requires considerable assistance" }
      "40":  { description: "Disabled, requires special care" }
      "30":  { description: "Severely disabled, hospitalization indicated" }
      "20":  { description: "Very sick, active supportive treatment" }
      "10":  { description: "Moribund" }
      "0":   { description: "Dead" }

  nyha:
    entity_aliases:
      - "NYHA"
      - "NYHA class"
      - "NYHA functional class"
      - "New York Heart Association"
      - "NYHA classification"
    loinc_code: "88020-3"
    unit_concept_id: 8527
    values:
      "1": { snomed_code: "420300004", description: "No limitation" }
      "2": { snomed_code: "421704003", description: "Slight limitation" }
      "3": { snomed_code: "420913000", description: "Marked limitation" }
      "4": { snomed_code: "422293003", description: "Unable without discomfort" }
```

Adding new scales (Child-Pugh, GCS, APACHE, etc.) is a YAML-only change — no code modifications needed.

## Step 2: `normalize_ordinal_value()` in `unit_normalizer.py`

New `@lru_cache` loader + two public functions. Existing APIs untouched.

```python
@lru_cache(maxsize=1)
def _load_ordinal_scales() -> tuple[dict[str, str], dict[str, Any]]:
    """Returns (alias_to_scale, scale_defs)."""
```

**Public function 1 — runtime normalization:**
```python
def normalize_ordinal_value(
    value_text: str | None,
    entity_text: str | None = None,
) -> tuple[int | None, int | None] | None:
    """Entity-context-aware ordinal value normalization.

    Returns:
        (value_concept_id, unit_concept_id) if entity matches an ordinal scale.
        value_concept_id is the approved OMOP ID from YAML (or None if not yet resolved).
        unit_concept_id is always 8527 ({score}) for ordinal scales.
        Returns None (not a tuple) if entity doesn't match any ordinal scale.
    """
```

Key behaviors:
- Entity matching: exact alias first, then bidirectional substring containment (case-insensitive)
- Value parsing: strips "grade"/"stage"/"class"/"score" prefixes, normalizes "2.0"→"2"
- Returns `omop_value_concept_id` from YAML if present, `None` if not yet resolved
- Always returns `unit_concept_id=8527` for recognized ordinal entities
- Returns Python `None` (not a tuple) for non-ordinal entities → signals fall-through

**Public function 2 — agent proposal utility:**
```python
def propose_ordinal_mappings() -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
    """Scan ordinal_scales config and return entries missing omop_value_concept_id.

    Returns a list of dicts, each with:
      - scale: scale key (e.g., "ecog")
      - grade: value string (e.g., "2")
      - snomed_code: known SNOMED code (if any)
      - loinc_answer: known LOINC answer code (if any)
      - description: human-readable description
      - status: "needs_resolution"

    This output can be fed to an LLM agent or OMOP vocabulary lookup to propose
    omop_value_concept_id values. Once approved, add them to the YAML.
    """
```

This is a read-only utility — it identifies gaps. A future agent (or manual Athena lookup) fills them. Once an `omop_value_concept_id` is added to the YAML and approved, the normalizer uses it automatically on next cache refresh.

## Step 3: Update `structure_builder.py` call site

In `_create_atomic_from_mapping()`, add ordinal-first dispatch:

```python
from protocol_processor.tools.unit_normalizer import (
    normalize_ordinal_value, normalize_unit, normalize_value,
)

# After _parse_value()...
raw_unit = fm.get("unit")
_, unit_concept_id = normalize_unit(raw_unit)

value_concept_id: int | None = None
entity_text = fm.get("entity")
raw_value = str(fm.get("value", ""))

# Ordinal-first: check if entity is an ordinal scale
ordinal_result = normalize_ordinal_value(raw_value, entity_text)
if ordinal_result is not None:
    ordinal_value_cid, ordinal_unit_cid = ordinal_result
    value_concept_id = ordinal_value_cid
    if ordinal_unit_cid is not None:
        unit_concept_id = ordinal_unit_cid
elif value_text and value_numeric is None:
    _, value_concept_id = normalize_value(value_text)
```

Result for ECOG <= 2: `value_numeric=2.0` (for comparison) + `unit_concept_id=8527` + `value_concept_id=<OMOP ID once approved, None until then>`.

## Step 4: Tests

**`test_unit_normalizer.py`** — Add `TestNormalizeOrdinalValue` (~18 tests):
- Entity matching: exact alias, full name, case-insensitive, substring ("ECOG PS")
- ECOG: all 6 grades recognized (unit_concept_id=8527 for each), grades are distinct where OMOP IDs exist
- Edge cases: "2.0"→"2", "Grade 2" prefix strip, unknown grade 7 → `(None, 8527)`
- Non-ordinal: "HbA1c" → `None`, None/empty entity → `None`
- `{score}` unit: `normalize_unit("score")` → 8527
- `propose_ordinal_mappings()`: returns entries missing OMOP IDs

**`test_phase3_integration.py`** — Update existing + add:
- ECOG criterion #11: `unit_concept_id` changes None → 8527
- Coverage counts updated
- New Karnofsky criterion: verify unit_concept_id=8527

1. `uv run pytest tests/test_unit_normalizer.py -v` — 30 existing + ~18 new pass
2. `uv run pytest tests/test_phase3_integration.py -v` — updated expectations pass
3. `uv run pytest tests/ -v` — full suite green
4. `uv run ruff check .` — clean
5. `propose_ordinal_mappings()` returns a list of entries needing OMOP ID resolution
6. After adding an OMOP ID to YAML → normalizer returns it on next invocation

```
     1→"""Unit and value normalizer: UCUM lookup with OMOP concept ID resolution.
     2→
     3→Phase 3 (Gap 7): Deterministic YAML-based lookup mapping common clinical
     4→trial units to UCUM codes and OMOP unit_concept_id values, plus optional
     5→categorical value normalization (e.g. "positive" -> SNOMED qualifier concept).
     6→
     7→No LLM calls — pure static lookup with alias expansion and case-insensitive
     8→matching.  Returns (None, None) for unrecognized inputs; never raises.
     9→"""
    10→
    11→from __future__ import annotations
    12→
    13→import logging
    14→from functools import lru_cache
    15→from pathlib import Path
    16→from typing import Any
    17→
    18→import yaml
    19→
    20→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    21→
    22→_CONFIG_PATH = Path(__file__).parent.parent / "config" / "ucum_mappings.yaml"
    23→
    24→
    25→@lru_cache(maxsize=1)
    26→def _load_ucum_mappings() -> tuple[dict[str, tuple[str, int]], dict[str, int]]:
    27→    """Load and index the UCUM mappings YAML (cached after first call).
    28→
    29→    Returns:
    30→        Tuple of (unit_lookup, value_lookup) where:
    31→        - unit_lookup: lowercased alias/canonical -> (canonical, omop_id)
    32→        - value_lookup: lowercased value text -> omop_value_concept_id
    33→    """
    34→    with open(_CONFIG_PATH) as f:
    35→        data: dict[str, Any] = yaml.safe_load(f)
    36→
    37→    unit_lookup: dict[str, tuple[str, int]] = {}
    38→    for entry in data.get("units", []):
    39→        canonical: str = entry["canonical"]
    40→        omop_id: int = entry["omop_unit_concept_id"]
    41→        # Index canonical form
    42→        unit_lookup[canonical.strip().lower()] = (canonical, omop_id)
    43→        # Index all aliases
    44→        for alias in entry.get("aliases", []):
    45→            unit_lookup[alias.strip().lower()] = (canonical, omop_id)
    46→
    47→    value_lookup: dict[str, int] = {}
    48→    for text, concept_id in data.get("value_mappings", {}).items():
    49→        value_lookup[str(text).strip().lower()] = int(concept_id)
    50→
    51→    return unit_lookup, value_lookup
    52→
    53→
    54→def normalize_unit(unit_text: str | None) -> tuple[str | None, int | None]:
    55→    """Normalize a unit string to its UCUM code and OMOP unit_concept_id.
    56→
    57→    Args:
    58→        unit_text: Raw unit text (e.g. "mg/dL", "%", "years").
    59→
    60→    Returns:
    61→        Tuple of (ucum_code, omop_unit_concept_id), or (None, None)
    62→        if the input is None, empty, or unrecognized.
    63→    """
    64→    if not unit_text or not unit_text.strip():
    65→        return (None, None)
    66→
    67→    key = unit_text.strip().lower()
    68→    unit_lookup, _ = _load_ucum_mappings()
    69→    result = unit_lookup.get(key)
    70→    if result is not None:
    71→        return result
    72→    return (None, None)
    73→
    74→
    75→def normalize_value(
    76→    value_text: str | None,
    77→) -> tuple[str | None, int | None]:
    78→    """Normalize a categorical value to its SNOMED code and OMOP concept ID.
    79→
    80→    Args:
    81→        value_text: Raw value text (e.g. "positive", "negative", "normal").
    82→
    83→    Returns:
    84→        Tuple of (normalized_text, omop_value_concept_id), or (None, None)
    85→        if the input is None, empty, or unrecognized.
    86→    """
    87→    if not value_text or not value_text.strip():
    88→        return (None, None)
    89→
    90→    key = value_text.strip().lower()
    91→    _, value_lookup = _load_ucum_mappings()
    92→    concept_id = value_lookup.get(key)
    93→    if concept_id is not None:
    94→        return (key, concept_id)
    95→    return (None, None)
    96→
```

```
     1→"""Structure builder tool for expression tree construction.
     2→
     3→Phase 2: Detects AND/OR/NOT logic in criterion text using Gemini,
     4→then builds normalized atomic_criteria/composite_criteria records
     5→and returns a StructuredCriterionTree for JSONB storage.
     6→
     7→Follows the field_mapper.py pattern: Gemini structured output with
     8→graceful fallback on failure.
     9→"""
    10→
    11→from __future__ import annotations
    12→
    13→import logging
    14→import os
    15→from typing import Any
    16→
    17→from shared.models import AtomicCriterion, CompositeCriterion, CriterionRelationship
    18→from sqlmodel import Session
    19→
    20→from protocol_processor.schemas.structure import (
    21→    ExpressionNode,
    22→    LogicDetectionResponse,
    23→    LogicNode,
    24→    StructuredCriterionTree,
    25→)
    26→from protocol_processor.tools.unit_normalizer import normalize_unit, normalize_value
    27→
    28→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    29→
    30→
    31→def _parse_value(
    32→    raw_value: str,
    33→) -> tuple[float | None, str | None]:
    34→    """Parse a value string into numeric or text.
    35→
    36→    Tries float conversion first. If it succeeds, returns (numeric, None).
    37→    Otherwise returns (None, text).
    38→
    39→    Args:
    40→        raw_value: Raw value string from field_mapping.
    41→
    42→    Returns:
    43→        Tuple of (value_numeric, value_text) — one is always None.
    44→    """
    45→    try:
    46→        return (float(raw_value), None)
    47→    except (ValueError, TypeError):
    48→        return (None, raw_value)
    49→
    50→
    51→async def detect_logic_structure(
    52→    criterion_text: str,
    53→    field_mappings: list[dict[str, Any]],
    54→) -> LogicDetectionResponse | None:
    55→    """Detect AND/OR/NOT logic structure in criterion text using Gemini.
    56→
    57→    Skips LLM call if there's only 1 field_mapping (single atom, no logic).
    58→    Returns None on any failure (triggers fallback in caller).
    59→
    60→    Args:
    61→        criterion_text: Full criterion text.
    62→        field_mappings: List of field_mapping dicts from conditions.
    63→
    64→    Returns:
    65→        LogicDetectionResponse with the detected tree, or None on failure.
    66→    """
    67→    if len(field_mappings) <= 1:
    68→        return None
    69→
    70→    google_api_key = os.getenv("GOOGLE_API_KEY")
    71→    if not google_api_key:
    72→        logger.warning(
    73→            "GOOGLE_API_KEY not set — skipping logic detection for '%s'",
    74→            criterion_text[:50],
    75→        )
    76→        return None
    77→
    78→    try:
    79→        from langchain_google_genai import ChatGoogleGenerativeAI
    80→
    81→        gemini_model_name = os.getenv("GEMINI_MODEL_NAME", "gemini-2.0-flash")
    82→        gemini = ChatGoogleGenerativeAI(
    83→            model=gemini_model_name,
    84→            google_api_key=google_api_key,
    85→        )
    86→        structured_llm = gemini.with_structured_output(LogicDetectionResponse)
    87→
    88→        # Build field mapping descriptions for context
    89→        mapping_lines = []
    90→        for i, fm in enumerate(field_mappings):
    91→            entity = fm.get("entity", "?")
    92→            relation = fm.get("relation", "?")
    93→            value = fm.get("value", "?")
    94→            unit = fm.get("unit", "")
    95→            unit_str = f" {unit}" if unit else ""
    96→            mapping_lines.append(f"  [{i}] {entity} {relation} {value}{unit_str}")
    97→        mappings_text = "\n".join(mapping_lines)
    98→
    99→        prompt = (
   100→            "You are a clinical trial protocol analyst. Analyze the logical"
   101→            " structure of this eligibility criterion.\n\n"
   102→            f"Criterion text: {criterion_text}\n\n"
   103→            f"Field mappings (indexed):\n{mappings_text}\n\n"
   104→            "Instructions:\n"
   105→            "- Determine how the field mappings are logically connected\n"
   106→            "- Use AND when all conditions must be met simultaneously\n"
   107→            "- Use OR when any one condition suffices\n"
   108→            "- Use NOT to negate a condition\n"
   109→            "- Use ATOMIC for leaf nodes that reference a single"
   110→            " field_mapping by index\n"
   111→            "- Return a tree where the root is AND, OR, NOT, or ATOMIC\n"
   112→            "- Every ATOMIC node must have field_mapping_index set to a"
   113→            " valid index (0 to "
   114→            f"{len(field_mappings) - 1})\n"
   115→            "- Each field_mapping index should appear exactly once\n"
   116→        )
   117→
   118→        result = structured_llm.invoke(prompt)
   119→        if isinstance(result, dict):
   120→            response = LogicDetectionResponse.model_validate(result)
   121→        else:
   122→            response = result  # type: ignore[assignment]
   123→
   124→        # Validate all field_mapping_index values are in range
   125→        if not _validate_logic_tree(response.root, len(field_mappings)):
   126→            logger.warning(
   127→                "Logic tree validation failed for '%s' — invalid indices",
   128→                criterion_text[:50],
   129→            )
   130→            return None
   131→
   132→        return response
   133→
   134→    except Exception as e:
   135→        logger.warning(
   136→            "Logic detection failed for '%s': %s",
   137→            criterion_text[:50],
   138→            e,
   139→            exc_info=True,
   140→        )
   141→        return None
   142→
   143→
   144→def _validate_logic_tree(node: LogicNode, num_mappings: int) -> bool:
   145→    """Validate that all field_mapping_index values are in range.
   146→
   147→    Args:
   148→        node: Root LogicNode to validate.
   149→        num_mappings: Number of field_mappings (valid range: 0..num_mappings-1).
   150→
   151→    Returns:
   152→        True if all indices are valid, False otherwise.
   153→    """
   154→    if node.node_type == "ATOMIC":
   155→        if node.field_mapping_index is None:
   156→            return False
   157→        return 0 <= node.field_mapping_index < num_mappings
   158→    if node.children:
   159→        return all(_validate_logic_tree(child, num_mappings) for child in node.children)
   160→    # Non-ATOMIC with no children is invalid (AND/OR/NOT need children)
   161→    return False
   162→
   163→
   164→def _create_atomic_from_mapping(
   165→    fm: dict[str, Any],
   166→    criterion_id: str,
   167→    protocol_id: str,
   168→    inclusion_exclusion: str,
   169→    criterion_text: str,
   170→) -> AtomicCriterion:
   171→    """Create an AtomicCriterion record from a field_mapping dict.
   172→
   173→    Args:
   174→        fm: Field mapping dict with entity, relation, value, unit keys.
   175→        criterion_id: FK to criteria table.
   176→        protocol_id: FK to protocol table.
   177→        inclusion_exclusion: "inclusion" or "exclusion".
   178→        criterion_text: Original criterion text.
   179→
   180→    Returns:
   181→        AtomicCriterion instance (not yet added to session).
   182→    """
   183→    value_str = fm.get("value", "")
   184→    value_numeric, value_text = _parse_value(value_str)
   185→
   186→    relation = fm.get("relation", "has")
   187→    negation = relation.upper() == "NOT" if relation else False
   188→
   189→    raw_unit = fm.get("unit")
   190→    _, unit_concept_id = normalize_unit(raw_unit)
   191→
   192→    value_concept_id: int | None = None
   193→    if value_text and value_numeric is None:
   194→        _, value_concept_id = normalize_value(value_text)
   195→
   196→    return AtomicCriterion(
   197→        criterion_id=criterion_id,
   198→        protocol_id=protocol_id,
   199→        inclusion_exclusion=inclusion_exclusion,
   200→        entity_concept_id=fm.get("entity_concept_id"),
   201→        entity_concept_system=fm.get("entity_concept_system"),
   202→        omop_concept_id=fm.get("omop_concept_id"),
   203→        entity_domain=fm.get("entity_domain"),
   204→        relation_operator=relation,
   205→        value_numeric=value_numeric,
   206→        value_text=value_text,
   207→        unit_text=raw_unit,
   208→        unit_concept_id=unit_concept_id,
   209→        value_concept_id=value_concept_id,
   210→        negation=negation,
   211→        original_text=criterion_text,
   212→        confidence_score=fm.get("confidence_score"),
   213→    )
   214→
   215→
   216→def _build_tree_from_logic(
   217→    node: LogicNode,
   218→    field_mappings: list[dict[str, Any]],
   219→    atomic_records: list[AtomicCriterion],
   220→    criterion_id: str,
   221→    protocol_id: str,
   222→    inclusion_exclusion: str,
   223→    session: Session,
   224→) -> ExpressionNode:
   225→    """Recursively build ExpressionNode tree and DB records from LogicNode.
   226→
   227→    Creates CompositeCriterion and CriterionRelationship records for
   228→    branch nodes. Leaf nodes reference already-created AtomicCriterion
   229→    records by index.
   230→
   231→    Args:
   232→        node: Current LogicNode from LLM output.
   233→        field_mappings: List of field_mapping dicts.
   234→        atomic_records: Pre-created AtomicCriterion records (indexed).
   235→        criterion_id: FK to criteria table.
   236→        protocol_id: FK to protocol table.
   237→        inclusion_exclusion: "inclusion" or "exclusion".
   238→        session: Active SQLModel session.
   239→
   240→    Returns:
   241→        ExpressionNode for JSONB storage.
   242→    """
   243→    if node.node_type == "ATOMIC":
   244→        idx = node.field_mapping_index or 0
   245→        atomic = atomic_records[idx]
   246→        fm = field_mappings[idx]
   247→        return ExpressionNode(
   248→            type="ATOMIC",
   249→            atomic_criterion_id=atomic.id,
   250→            entity=fm.get("entity"),
   251→            relation=fm.get("relation"),
   252→            value=fm.get("value"),
   253→            unit=fm.get("unit"),
   254→            omop_concept_id=fm.get("omop_concept_id"),
   255→        )
   256→
   257→    # Branch node: create CompositeCriterion
   258→    composite = CompositeCriterion(
   259→        criterion_id=criterion_id,
   260→        protocol_id=protocol_id,
   261→        inclusion_exclusion=inclusion_exclusion,
   262→        logic_operator=node.node_type,
   263→    )
   264→    session.add(composite)
   265→    session.flush()
   266→
   267→    children_nodes: list[ExpressionNode] = []
   268→    for seq, child in enumerate(node.children or []):
   269→        child_expr = _build_tree_from_logic(
   270→            child,
   271→            field_mappings,
   272→            atomic_records,
   273→            criterion_id,
   274→            protocol_id,
   275→            inclusion_exclusion,
   276→            session,
   277→        )
   278→        children_nodes.append(child_expr)
   279→
   280→        # Create relationship edge
   281→        if child.node_type == "ATOMIC":
   282→            child_id = child_expr.atomic_criterion_id or ""
   283→            child_type = "atomic"
   284→        else:
   285→            # For composite children, the CompositeCriterion was just
   286→            # created in the recursive call — we need its ID.
   287→            # We stored it in the ExpressionNode via a convention:
   288→            # non-ATOMIC nodes don't have atomic_criterion_id set.
   289→            # Query the last composite created for this criterion.
   290→            child_id = _find_last_composite_id(session, criterion_id, child.node_type)
   291→            child_type = "composite"
   292→
   293→        rel = CriterionRelationship(
   294→            parent_criterion_id=composite.id,
   295→            child_criterion_id=child_id,
   296→            child_type=child_type,
   297→            child_sequence=seq,
   298→        )
   299→        session.add(rel)
   300→
   301→    return ExpressionNode(
   302→        type=node.node_type,
   303→        children=children_nodes,
   304→    )
   305→
   306→
   307→def _find_last_composite_id(
   308→    session: Session, criterion_id: str, logic_operator: str
   309→) -> str:
   310→    """Find the most recently added composite criterion ID.
   311→
   312→    This is used during recursive tree building to link parent→child
   313→    composite relationships.
   314→
   315→    Args:
   316→        session: Active SQLModel session.
   317→        criterion_id: FK to criteria table.
   318→        logic_operator: Logic operator of the child composite.
   319→
   320→    Returns:
   321→        ID of the most recent CompositeCriterion matching the criteria.
   322→    """
   323→    from sqlmodel import select
   324→
   325→    stmt = (
   326→        select(CompositeCriterion)
   327→        .where(CompositeCriterion.criterion_id == criterion_id)
   328→        .where(CompositeCriterion.logic_operator == logic_operator)
   329→        .order_by(CompositeCriterion.created_at.desc())  # type: ignore[attr-defined]
   330→    )
   331→    result = session.exec(stmt).first()
   332→    return result.id if result else ""
   333→
   334→
   335→async def build_expression_tree(
   336→    criterion_text: str,
   337→    field_mappings: list[dict[str, Any]],
   338→    criterion_id: str,
   339→    protocol_id: str,
   340→    inclusion_exclusion: str,
   341→    session: Session,
   342→) -> StructuredCriterionTree:
   343→    """Build an expression tree from criterion field_mappings.
   344→
   345→    Two-pass approach:
   346→    1. LLM pass: call detect_logic_structure() for AND/OR/NOT detection
   347→    2. Fallback: if LLM fails or single mapping, AND-of-all-atomics
   348→
   349→    Creates AtomicCriterion records for each field_mapping, plus
   350→    CompositeCriterion + CriterionRelationship for tree structure.
   351→
   352→    Args:
   353→        criterion_text: Full criterion text.
   354→        field_mappings: List of field_mapping dicts from conditions.
   355→        criterion_id: FK to criteria table.
   356→        protocol_id: FK to protocol table.
   357→        inclusion_exclusion: "inclusion" or "exclusion".
   358→        session: Active SQLModel session (records are added but not committed).
   359→
   360→    Returns:
   361→        StructuredCriterionTree for JSONB storage in criteria.structured_criterion.
   362→    """
   363→    gemini_model_name = os.getenv("GEMINI_MODEL_NAME", "gemini-2.0-flash")
   364→
   365→    # Create AtomicCriterion records for each field_mapping
   366→    atomic_records: list[AtomicCriterion] = []
   367→    for fm in field_mappings:
   368→        atomic = _create_atomic_from_mapping(
   369→            fm, criterion_id, protocol_id, inclusion_exclusion, criterion_text
   370→        )
   371→        session.add(atomic)
   372→        session.flush()
   373→        atomic_records.append(atomic)
   374→
   375→    # Pass 1: LLM logic detection
   376→    logic_response = await detect_logic_structure(criterion_text, field_mappings)
   377→
   378→    if logic_response is not None:
   379→        # Build tree from LLM-detected structure
   380→        root_expr = _build_tree_from_logic(
   381→            logic_response.root,
   382→            field_mappings,
   383→            atomic_records,
   384→            criterion_id,
   385→            protocol_id,
   386→            inclusion_exclusion,
   387→            session,
   388→        )
   389→        return StructuredCriterionTree(
   390→            root=root_expr,
   391→            structure_confidence="llm",
   392→            structure_model=gemini_model_name,
   393→        )
   394→
   395→    # Pass 2: Fallback — AND-of-all-atomics (or single ATOMIC)
   396→    if len(atomic_records) == 1:
   397→        fm = field_mappings[0]
   398→        root_expr = ExpressionNode(
   399→            type="ATOMIC",
   400→            atomic_criterion_id=atomic_records[0].id,
   401→            entity=fm.get("entity"),
   402→            relation=fm.get("relation"),
   403→            value=fm.get("value"),
   404→            unit=fm.get("unit"),
   405→            omop_concept_id=fm.get("omop_concept_id"),
   406→        )
   407→        return StructuredCriterionTree(
   408→            root=root_expr,
   409→            structure_confidence="fallback",
   410→            structure_model=None,
   411→        )
   412→
   413→    # Multiple mappings, LLM failed → wrap in AND
   414→    composite = CompositeCriterion(
   415→        criterion_id=criterion_id,
   416→        protocol_id=protocol_id,
   417→        inclusion_exclusion=inclusion_exclusion,
   418→        logic_operator="AND",
   419→        original_text=criterion_text,
   420→    )
   421→    session.add(composite)
   422→    session.flush()
   423→
   424→    children: list[ExpressionNode] = []
   425→    for seq, (atomic, fm) in enumerate(zip(atomic_records, field_mappings)):
   426→        children.append(
   427→            ExpressionNode(
   428→                type="ATOMIC",
   429→                atomic_criterion_id=atomic.id,
   430→                entity=fm.get("entity"),
   431→                relation=fm.get("relation"),
   432→                value=fm.get("value"),
   433→                unit=fm.get("unit"),
   434→                omop_concept_id=fm.get("omop_concept_id"),
   435→            )
   436→        )
   437→        rel = CriterionRelationship(
   438→            parent_criterion_id=composite.id,
   439→            child_criterion_id=atomic.id,
   440→            child_type="atomic",
   441→            child_sequence=seq,
   442→        )
   443→        session.add(rel)
   444→
   445→    root_expr = ExpressionNode(type="AND", children=children)
   446→    return StructuredCriterionTree(
   447→        root=root_expr,
   448→        structure_confidence="fallback",
   449→        structure_model=None,
   450→    )
   451→
```

```
     1→"""Tests for Phase 3: Unit and Value Normalizer.
     2→
     3→Tests normalize_unit() and normalize_value() from the UCUM YAML lookup.
     4→Covers canonical forms, aliases, case insensitivity, whitespace, None/empty,
     5→and unrecognized inputs.
     6→"""
     7→
     8→from __future__ import annotations
     9→
    10→from protocol_processor.tools.unit_normalizer import normalize_unit, normalize_value
    11→
    12→# ===========================================================================
    13→# normalize_unit() tests
    14→# ===========================================================================
    15→
    16→
    17→class TestNormalizeUnit:
    18→    """Tests for normalize_unit() — UCUM code + OMOP unit_concept_id lookup."""
    19→
    20→    def test_canonical_percent(self) -> None:
    21→        """Canonical '%' resolves to OMOP 8554."""
    22→        ucum, omop_id = normalize_unit("%")
    23→        assert ucum == "%"
    24→        assert omop_id == 8554
    25→
    26→    def test_alias_percent(self) -> None:
    27→        """Alias 'percent' resolves to '%' / 8554."""
    28→        ucum, omop_id = normalize_unit("percent")
    29→        assert ucum == "%"
    30→        assert omop_id == 8554
    31→
    32→    def test_canonical_mg_dl(self) -> None:
    33→        """Canonical 'mg/dL' resolves to OMOP 8840."""
    34→        ucum, omop_id = normalize_unit("mg/dL")
    35→        assert ucum == "mg/dL"
    36→        assert omop_id == 8840
    37→
    38→    def test_alias_mg_dl_lowercase(self) -> None:
    39→        """Alias 'mg/dl' resolves to mg/dL / 8840."""
    40→        ucum, omop_id = normalize_unit("mg/dl")
    41→        assert ucum == "mg/dL"
    42→        assert omop_id == 8840
    43→
    44→    def test_years_alias(self) -> None:
    45→        """Alias 'years' resolves to 'a' / 9448."""
    46→        ucum, omop_id = normalize_unit("years")
    47→        assert ucum == "a"
    48→        assert omop_id == 9448
    49→
    50→    def test_yr_alias(self) -> None:
    51→        """Alias 'yr' resolves to 'a' / 9448."""
    52→        ucum, omop_id = normalize_unit("yr")
    53→        assert ucum == "a"
    54→        assert omop_id == 9448
    55→
    56→    def test_ml_min(self) -> None:
    57→        """'mL/min' resolves to OMOP 8795."""
    58→        ucum, omop_id = normalize_unit("mL/min")
    59→        assert ucum == "mL/min"
    60→        assert omop_id == 8795
    61→
    62→    def test_kg_m2(self) -> None:
    63→        """'kg/m2' resolves to OMOP 9531."""
    64→        ucum, omop_id = normalize_unit("kg/m2")
    65→        assert ucum == "kg/m2"
    66→        assert omop_id == 9531
    67→
    68→    def test_mmhg_alias(self) -> None:
    69→        """'mmHg' alias resolves to 'mm[Hg]' / 8876."""
    70→        ucum, omop_id = normalize_unit("mmHg")
    71→        assert ucum == "mm[Hg]"
    72→        assert omop_id == 8876
    73→
    74→    def test_mmol_l(self) -> None:
    75→        """'mmol/L' resolves to OMOP 8753."""
    76→        ucum, omop_id = normalize_unit("mmol/L")
    77→        assert ucum == "mmol/L"
    78→        assert omop_id == 8753
    79→
    80→    def test_ml_min_173m2(self) -> None:
    81→        """'mL/min/1.73m2' resolves to OMOP 720870."""
    82→        ucum, omop_id = normalize_unit("mL/min/1.73m2")
    83→        assert ucum == "mL/min/1.73m2"
    84→        assert omop_id == 720870
    85→
    86→    def test_cell_count_10_3_ul(self) -> None:
    87→        """'10*3/uL' resolves to OMOP 8848."""
    88→        ucum, omop_id = normalize_unit("10*3/uL")
    89→        assert ucum == "10*3/uL"
    90→        assert omop_id == 8848
    91→
    92→    def test_cell_count_10_9_l(self) -> None:
    93→        """'10*9/L' resolves to OMOP 9444."""
    94→        ucum, omop_id = normalize_unit("10*9/L")
    95→        assert ucum == "10*9/L"
    96→        assert omop_id == 9444
    97→
    98→    def test_case_insensitive(self) -> None:
    99→        """Lookup should be case-insensitive."""
   100→        ucum, omop_id = normalize_unit("MG/DL")
   101→        assert ucum == "mg/dL"
   102→        assert omop_id == 8840
   103→
   104→    def test_whitespace_stripped(self) -> None:
   105→        """Leading/trailing whitespace should be stripped."""
   106→        ucum, omop_id = normalize_unit("  %  ")
   107→        assert ucum == "%"
   108→        assert omop_id == 8554
   109→
   110→    def test_none_input(self) -> None:
   111→        """None input returns (None, None)."""
   112→        assert normalize_unit(None) == (None, None)
   113→
   114→    def test_empty_string(self) -> None:
   115→        """Empty string returns (None, None)."""
   116→        assert normalize_unit("") == (None, None)
   117→
   118→    def test_whitespace_only(self) -> None:
   119→        """Whitespace-only string returns (None, None)."""
   120→        assert normalize_unit("   ") == (None, None)
   121→
   122→    def test_unrecognized_unit(self) -> None:
   123→        """Unrecognized unit returns (None, None)."""
   124→        assert normalize_unit("foobar_unit") == (None, None)
   125→
   126→

   130→
   131→
   132→class TestNormalizeValue:
   133→    """Tests for normalize_value() — categorical value normalization."""
   134→
   135→    def test_positive(self) -> None:
   136→        """'positive' resolves to 45884084."""
   137→        text, omop_id = normalize_value("positive")
   138→        assert text == "positive"
   139→        assert omop_id == 45884084
   140→
   141→    def test_negative(self) -> None:
   142→        """'negative' resolves to 45878583."""
   143→        text, omop_id = normalize_value("negative")
   144→        assert text == "negative"
   145→        assert omop_id == 45878583
   146→
   147→    def test_normal(self) -> None:
   148→        """'normal' resolves to 45884153."""
   149→        text, omop_id = normalize_value("normal")
   150→        assert text == "normal"
   151→        assert omop_id == 45884153
   152→
   153→    def test_abnormal(self) -> None:
   154→        """'abnormal' resolves to 45878745."""
   155→        text, omop_id = normalize_value("abnormal")
   156→        assert text == "abnormal"
   157→        assert omop_id == 45878745
   158→
   159→    def test_present_maps_to_positive(self) -> None:
   160→        """'present' maps to same concept as 'positive' (45884084)."""
   161→        text, omop_id = normalize_value("present")
   162→        assert text == "present"
   163→        assert omop_id == 45884084
   164→
   165→    def test_absent_maps_to_negative(self) -> None:
   166→        """'absent' maps to same concept as 'negative' (45878583)."""
   167→        text, omop_id = normalize_value("absent")
   168→        assert text == "absent"
   169→        assert omop_id == 45878583
   170→
   171→    def test_case_insensitive(self) -> None:
   172→        """Value lookup is case-insensitive."""
   173→        text, omop_id = normalize_value("POSITIVE")
   174→        assert text == "positive"
   175→        assert omop_id == 45884084
   176→
   177→    def test_whitespace_stripped(self) -> None:
   178→        """Leading/trailing whitespace is stripped."""
   179→        text, omop_id = normalize_value("  negative  ")
   180→        assert text == "negative"
   181→        assert omop_id == 45878583
   182→
   183→    def test_none_input(self) -> None:
   184→        """None input returns (None, None)."""
   185→        assert normalize_value(None) == (None, None)
   186→
   187→    def test_empty_string(self) -> None:
   188→        """Empty string returns (None, None)."""
   189→        assert normalize_value("") == (None, None)
   190→
   191→    def test_unrecognized_value(self) -> None:
   192→        """Unrecognized value returns (None, None)."""
   193→        assert normalize_value("borderline") == (None, None)
```

```
     1→"""Phase 3 Integration: Run realistic clinical criteria through the backend.
     2→
     3→Feeds representative inclusion/exclusion criteria (as field_mappings) through
     4→build_expression_tree and verifies that entities, relations, values, units,
     5→unit_concept_id, and value_concept_id are all correctly populated in the DB.
     6→
     7→These field_mappings simulate what the ground node would produce for real
     8→clinical trial protocols.
     9→"""
    10→
    11→from __future__ import annotations
    12→
    13→import os
    14→from typing import Any, Generator
    15→from unittest.mock import patch
    16→
    17→import pytest
    18→from shared.models import AtomicCriterion, Criteria, CriteriaBatch, Protocol
    19→from sqlalchemy.pool import StaticPool
    20→from sqlmodel import Session, SQLModel, create_engine, select

    23→
    24→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    25→# Fixtures
    26→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

    28→

    31→    eng = create_engine(
    32→        "sqlite:///:memory:",
    33→        connect_args={"check_same_thread": False},
    34→        poolclass=StaticPool,
    35→    )
    36→    import shared.models  # noqa: F401
    37→
    38→    SQLModel.metadata.create_all(eng)
    39→    try:
    40→        yield eng
    41→    finally:
    42→        eng.dispose()
    43→
    44→
    45→@pytest.fixture()
    46→def session(engine) -> Generator[Session, None, None]:
    47→    with Session(engine) as s:
    48→        yield s
    49→
    50→
    51→def _setup_parent(
    52→    session: Session,
    53→) -> tuple[str, str, str]:
    54→    """Create Protocol + CriteriaBatch, return (protocol_id, batch_id)."""
    55→    protocol = Protocol(title="NCT00001234", file_uri="local://test.pdf")
    56→    session.add(protocol)
    57→    session.flush()
    58→    batch = CriteriaBatch(protocol_id=protocol.id)
    59→    session.add(batch)
    60→    session.flush()
    61→    return protocol.id, batch.id
    62→
    63→
    64→def _make_criterion(
    65→    session: Session,
    66→    batch_id: str,
    67→    text: str,
    68→    criteria_type: str = "inclusion",
    69→) -> Criteria:
    70→    crit = Criteria(batch_id=batch_id, criteria_type=criteria_type, text=text)
    71→    session.add(crit)
    72→    session.flush()
    73→    return crit
    74→

   445→# ===========================================================================
   446→# Tests
   447→# ===========================================================================

   449→
   450→class TestPhase3RealisticCriteria:
   451→    """Run 12 realistic clinical trial criteria through the backend."""
   452→
   453→    async def test_all_criteria_processed(self, session) -> None:
   454→        """All 12 criteria produce correct AtomicCriterion records."""
   455→        protocol_id, batch_id = _setup_parent(session)
   456→        all_atomics: list[AtomicCriterion] = []
   457→
   458→        with patch.dict(os.environ, {}, clear=False):
   459→            os.environ.pop("GOOGLE_API_KEY", None)
   460→
   461→            for crit_def in CRITERIA:
   462→                crit = _make_criterion(
   463→                    session,
   464→                    batch_id,
   465→                    crit_def["text"],
   466→                    crit_def["type"],
   467→                )
   468→                await build_expression_tree(
   469→                    criterion_text=crit_def["text"],
   470→                    field_mappings=crit_def["mappings"],
   471→                    criterion_id=crit.id,
   472→                    protocol_id=protocol_id,
   473→                    inclusion_exclusion=crit_def["type"],
   474→                    session=session,
   475→                )
   476→                session.flush()
   477→
   478→                # Collect atomics for this criterion
   479→                atomics = session.exec(
   480→                    select(AtomicCriterion).where(
   481→                        AtomicCriterion.criterion_id == crit.id
   482→                    )
   483→                ).all()
   484→                all_atomics.extend(atomics)
   485→
   486→        session.commit()
   487→
   488→        # Total expected: sum of mappings across all criteria
   489→        total_mappings = sum(len(c["mappings"]) for c in CRITERIA)
   490→        assert len(all_atomics) == total_mappings, (
   491→            f"Expected {total_mappings} atomics, got {len(all_atomics)}"
   492→        )
   493→
   494→        # Verify each atomic against expected values
   495→        for i, (atomic, exp) in enumerate(zip(all_atomics, EXPECTED)):
   496→            label = f"Atomic #{i}"
   497→            assert atomic.entity_concept_id == exp["entity_concept_id"], (
   498→                f"{label}: entity_concept_id"
   499→            )
   500→            assert atomic.relation_operator == exp["relation_operator"], (
   501→                f"{label}: relation_operator"
   502→            )
   503→            assert atomic.unit_text == exp.get("unit_text"), f"{label}: unit_text"
   504→            assert atomic.unit_concept_id == exp["unit_concept_id"], (
   505→                f"{label}: unit_concept_id "
   506→                f"(got {atomic.unit_concept_id}, "
   507→                f"expected {exp['unit_concept_id']} "
   508→                f"for unit '{atomic.unit_text}')"
   509→            )
   510→            assert atomic.value_concept_id == exp["value_concept_id"], (
   511→                f"{label}: value_concept_id "
   512→                f"(got {atomic.value_concept_id}, "
   513→                f"expected {exp['value_concept_id']} "
   514→                f"for value '{atomic.value_text}')"
   515→            )
   516→            if exp.get("value_numeric") is not None:
   517→                assert atomic.value_numeric == pytest.approx(exp["value_numeric"]), (
   518→                    f"{label}: value_numeric"
   519→                )
   520→            else:
   521→                assert atomic.value_numeric is None, (
   522→                    f"{label}: value_numeric should be None"
   523→                )
   524→            if "value_text" in exp:
   525→                assert atomic.value_text == exp["value_text"], f"{label}: value_text"
   526→
   527→    async def test_inclusion_exclusion_correct(self, session) -> None:
   528→        """Criteria correctly tagged as inclusion or exclusion."""
   529→        protocol_id, batch_id = _setup_parent(session)
   530→
   531→        with patch.dict(os.environ, {}, clear=False):
   532→            os.environ.pop("GOOGLE_API_KEY", None)
   533→
   534→            for crit_def in CRITERIA:
   535→                crit = _make_criterion(
   536→                    session,
   537→                    batch_id,
   538→                    crit_def["text"],
   539→                    crit_def["type"],
   540→                )
   541→                await build_expression_tree(
   542→                    criterion_text=crit_def["text"],
   543→                    field_mappings=crit_def["mappings"],
   544→                    criterion_id=crit.id,
   545→                    protocol_id=protocol_id,
   546→                    inclusion_exclusion=crit_def["type"],
   547→                    session=session,
   548→                )
   549→
   550→        session.commit()
   551→
   552→        inclusion_atomics = session.exec(
   553→            select(AtomicCriterion).where(
   554→                AtomicCriterion.inclusion_exclusion == "inclusion"
   555→            )
   556→        ).all()
   557→        exclusion_atomics = session.exec(
   558→            select(AtomicCriterion).where(
   559→                AtomicCriterion.inclusion_exclusion == "exclusion"
   560→            )
   561→        ).all()
   562→
   563→        # Criteria 7 and 8 are exclusion (1 mapping each = 2 atomics)
   564→        assert len(exclusion_atomics) == 2
   565→        # All others are inclusion (13 mappings total)
   566→        assert len(inclusion_atomics) == 13
   567→
   568→    async def test_unit_coverage_summary(self, session) -> None:
   569→        """Print a coverage summary of all units processed."""
   570→        protocol_id, batch_id = _setup_parent(session)
   571→
   572→        with patch.dict(os.environ, {}, clear=False):
   573→            os.environ.pop("GOOGLE_API_KEY", None)
   574→
   575→            for crit_def in CRITERIA:
   576→                crit = _make_criterion(
   577→                    session,
   578→                    batch_id,
   579→                    crit_def["text"],
   580→                    crit_def["type"],
   581→                )
   582→                await build_expression_tree(
   583→                    criterion_text=crit_def["text"],
   584→                    field_mappings=crit_def["mappings"],
   585→                    criterion_id=crit.id,
   586→                    protocol_id=protocol_id,
   587→                    inclusion_exclusion=crit_def["type"],
   588→                    session=session,
   589→                )
   590→
   591→        session.commit()
   592→
   593→        all_atomics = session.exec(select(AtomicCriterion)).all()
   594→
   595→        resolved = [a for a in all_atomics if a.unit_concept_id is not None]
   596→        unresolved_with_unit = [
   597→            a
   598→            for a in all_atomics
   599→            if a.unit_text is not None and a.unit_concept_id is None
   600→        ]
   601→        no_unit = [a for a in all_atomics if a.unit_text is None]
   602→        value_resolved = [a for a in all_atomics if a.value_concept_id is not None]
   603→
   604→        # Verify we got good coverage
   605→        # 12 mappings have recognized units (%, years, mL/min/1.73m2,
   606→        # kg/m2, mmHg, 10*3/uL, mg/dL, mmol/L, 10*9/L)
   607→        assert len(resolved) == 12
   608→        assert len(unresolved_with_unit) == 0  # no unrecognized units
   609→        assert len(no_unit) == 3  # HIV, HBsAg (None), ECOG (None)
   610→        assert len(value_resolved) == 2  # positive + negative
   611→
```

```json
{
```

```json
  "old_string": "\"\"\"Unit and value normalizer: UCUM lookup with OMOP concept ID resolution.\n\nPhase 3 (Gap 7): Deterministic YAML-based lookup mapping common clinical\ntrial units to UCUM codes and OMOP unit_concept_id values, plus optional\ncategorical value normalization (e.g. \"positive\" -> SNOMED qualifier concept).\n\nNo LLM calls — pure static lookup with alias expansion and case-insensitive\nmatching.  Returns (None, None) for unrecognized inputs; never raises.\n\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport logging\nfrom functools import lru_cache\nfrom pathlib import Path\nfrom typing import Any\n\nimport yaml\n\nlogger = logging.getLogger(__name__)\n\n_CONFIG_PATH = Path(__file__).parent.parent / \"config\" / \"ucum_mappings.yaml\"",
  "new_string": "\"\"\"Unit and value normalizer: UCUM lookup with OMOP concept ID resolution.\n\nPhase 3 (Gap 7): Deterministic YAML-based lookup mapping common clinical\ntrial units to UCUM codes and OMOP unit_concept_id values, plus optional\ncategorical value normalization (e.g. \"positive\" -> SNOMED qualifier concept).\n\nPhase 3b: Ordinal scale normalization (ECOG, Karnofsky, NYHA, etc.) with\nagent-assisted concept resolution.  Recognized ordinal entities get\nunit_concept_id=8527 ({score}); value_concept_id comes from YAML config\n(populated incrementally via agent/approval workflow).\n\nNo LLM calls — pure static lookup with alias expansion and case-insensitive\nmatching.  Returns (None, None) for unrecognized inputs; never raises.\n\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport logging\nimport re\nfrom functools import lru_cache\nfrom pathlib import Path\nfrom typing import Any\n\nimport yaml\n\nlogger = logging.getLogger(__name__)\n\n_CONFIG_PATH = Path(__file__).parent.parent / \"config\" / \"ucum_mappings.yaml\"\n\n_ORDINAL_PREFIX_RE = re.compile(\n    r\"^(?:grade|stage|class|score|level)\\s*\",\n    re.IGNORECASE,\n)"
}
```

```json
{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/noahdolevelixir/Code/medgemma-hackathon/services/protocol-processor-service/src/protocol_processor/tools/unit_normalizer.py",
  "old_string": "    key = value_text.strip().lower()\n    _, value_lookup = _load_ucum_mappings()\n    concept_id = value_lookup.get(key)\n    if concept_id is not None:\n        return (key, concept_id)\n    return (None, None)",
  "new_string": "    key = value_text.strip().lower()\n    _, value_lookup = _load_ucum_mappings()\n    concept_id = value_lookup.get(key)\n    if concept_id is not None:\n        return (key, concept_id)\n    return (None, None)\n\n\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# Ordinal scale normalization (Phase 3b)\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n\n@lru_cache(maxsize=1)\ndef _load_ordinal_scales() -> tuple[dict[str, str], dict[str, Any]]:\n    \"\"\"Load ordinal scale definitions from YAML config (cached).\n\n    Returns:\n        Tuple of (alias_to_scale, scale_defs) where:\n        - alias_to_scale: lowercased alias -> scale key (e.g. \"ecog\")\n        - scale_defs: raw ordinal_scales dict from YAML\n    \"\"\"\n    with open(_CONFIG_PATH) as f:\n        data: dict[str, Any] = yaml.safe_load(f)\n\n    scale_defs: dict[str, Any] = data.get(\"ordinal_scales\", {})\n    alias_to_scale: dict[str, str] = {}\n    for scale_key, scale_def in scale_defs.items():\n        for alias in scale_def.get(\"entity_aliases\", []):\n            alias_to_scale[alias.strip().lower()] = scale_key\n\n    return alias_to_scale, scale_defs\n\n\ndef _match_ordinal_scale(entity_text: str) -> str | None:\n    \"\"\"Match entity text to an ordinal scale key.\n\n    Uses three strategies in order:\n    1. Exact alias match (case-insensitive)\n    2. Entity text is a substring of an alias\n    3. An alias is a substring of entity text\n\n    Returns:\n        Scale key (e.g. \"ecog\") or None if no match.\n    \"\"\"\n    alias_to_scale, _ = _load_ordinal_scales()\n    key = entity_text.strip().lower()\n\n    # Strategy 1: exact alias match\n    if key in alias_to_scale:\n        return alias_to_scale[key]\n\n    # Strategy 2 & 3: bidirectional substring containment\n    for alias, scale_key in alias_to_scale.items():\n        if key in alias or alias in key:\n            return scale_key\n\n    return None\n\n\ndef normalize_ordinal_value(\n    value_text: str | None,\n    entity_text: str | None = None,\n) -> tuple[int | None, int | None] | None:\n    \"\"\"Entity-context-aware ordinal value normalization.\n\n    Returns:\n        (value_concept_id, unit_concept_id) if entity matches an ordinal scale.\n        value_concept_id is the approved OMOP ID from YAML (or None if not yet\n        resolved).  unit_concept_id is always 8527 ({score}) for ordinal scales.\n        Returns None (not a tuple) if entity doesn't match any ordinal scale.\n    \"\"\"\n    if not entity_text or not entity_text.strip():\n        return None\n\n    scale_key = _match_ordinal_scale(entity_text)\n    if scale_key is None:\n        return None\n\n    _, scale_defs = _load_ordinal_scales()\n    scale_def = scale_defs[scale_key]\n    unit_concept_id: int | None = scale_def.get(\"unit_concept_id\")\n\n    if not value_text or not value_text.strip():\n        return (None, unit_concept_id)\n\n    # Normalize value: strip ordinal prefixes, convert \"2.0\" -> \"2\"\n    cleaned = _ORDINAL_PREFIX_RE.sub(\"\", value_text.strip())\n    try:\n        numeric = float(cleaned)\n        if numeric == int(numeric):\n            cleaned = str(int(numeric))\n        else:\n            cleaned = str(numeric)\n    except (ValueError, TypeError):\n        cleaned = cleaned.strip()\n\n    values = scale_def.get(\"values\", {})\n    val_entry = values.get(cleaned)\n    if val_entry is None:\n        # Grade not in the defined values for this scale\n        return (None, unit_concept_id)\n\n    omop_value_cid = val_entry.get(\"omop_value_concept_id\")\n    return (omop_value_cid, unit_concept_id)\n\n\ndef propose_ordinal_mappings() -> list[dict[str, Any]]:\n    \"\"\"Scan ordinal_scales config and return entries missing omop_value_concept_id.\n\n    Returns a list of dicts, each with:\n      - scale: scale key (e.g., \"ecog\")\n      - grade: value string (e.g., \"2\")\n      - snomed_code: known SNOMED code (if any)\n      - loinc_answer: known LOINC answer code (if any)\n      - description: human-readable description\n      - status: \"needs_resolution\"\n\n    This output can be fed to an LLM agent or OMOP vocabulary lookup to propose\n    omop_value_concept_id values.  Once approved, add them to the YAML.\n    \"\"\"\n    _, scale_defs = _load_ordinal_scales()\n    missing: list[dict[str, Any]] = []\n\n    for scale_key, scale_def in scale_defs.items():\n        for grade, val_entry in scale_def.get(\"values\", {}).items():\n            if val_entry.get(\"omop_value_concept_id\") is not None:\n                continue\n            missing.append(\n                {\n                    \"scale\": scale_key,\n                    \"grade\": grade,\n                    \"snomed_code\": val_entry.get(\"snomed_code\"),\n                    \"loinc_answer\": val_entry.get(\"loinc_answer\"),\n                    \"description\": val_entry.get(\"description\"),\n                    \"status\": \"needs_resolution\",\n                }\n            )\n\n    return missing"
}
```

```json
  "old_string": "    value_str = fm.get(\"value\", \"\")\n    value_numeric, value_text = _parse_value(value_str)\n\n    relation = fm.get(\"relation\", \"has\")\n    negation = relation.upper() == \"NOT\" if relation else False\n\n    raw_unit = fm.get(\"unit\")\n    _, unit_concept_id = normalize_unit(raw_unit)\n\n    value_concept_id: int | None = None\n    if value_text and value_numeric is None:\n        _, value_concept_id = normalize_value(value_text)",
  "new_string": "    value_str = fm.get(\"value\", \"\")\n    value_numeric, value_text = _parse_value(value_str)\n\n    relation = fm.get(\"relation\", \"has\")\n    negation = relation.upper() == \"NOT\" if relation else False\n\n    raw_unit = fm.get(\"unit\")\n    _, unit_concept_id = normalize_unit(raw_unit)\n\n    value_concept_id: int | None = None\n    entity_text = fm.get(\"entity\")\n    raw_value = str(fm.get(\"value\", \"\"))\n\n    # Ordinal-first: check if entity is an ordinal scale\n    ordinal_result = normalize_ordinal_value(raw_value, entity_text)\n    if ordinal_result is not None:\n        ordinal_value_cid, ordinal_unit_cid = ordinal_result\n        value_concept_id = ordinal_value_cid\n        if ordinal_unit_cid is not None:\n            unit_concept_id = ordinal_unit_cid\n    elif value_text and value_numeric is None:\n        _, value_concept_id = normalize_value(value_text)"
}
```

```json
  "new_string": "    def test_unrecognized_value(self) -> None:\n        \"\"\"Unrecognized value returns (None, None).\"\"\"\n        assert normalize_value(\"borderline\") == (None, None)\n\n\n# ===========================================================================\n# normalize_ordinal_value() tests (Phase 3b)\n# ===========================================================================\n\n\nclass TestNormalizeOrdinalValue:\n    \"\"\"Tests for normalize_ordinal_value() — ordinal scale normalization.\"\"\"\n\n    # --- Entity matching ---\n\n    def test_exact_alias_ecog(self) -> None:\n        \"\"\"Exact alias 'ECOG' matches the ecog scale.\"\"\"\n        result = normalize_ordinal_value(\"2\", \"ECOG\")\n        assert result is not None\n        _, unit_cid = result\n        assert unit_cid == 8527\n\n    def test_full_name_ecog(self) -> None:\n        \"\"\"Full name 'ECOG performance status' matches.\"\"\"\n        result = normalize_ordinal_value(\"1\", \"ECOG performance status\")\n        assert result is not None\n        _, unit_cid = result\n        assert unit_cid == 8527\n\n    def test_case_insensitive_ecog(self) -> None:\n        \"\"\"Case-insensitive matching: 'ecog' matches.\"\"\"\n        result = normalize_ordinal_value(\"0\", \"ecog\")\n        assert result is not None\n        _, unit_cid = result\n        assert unit_cid == 8527\n\n    def test_substring_ecog_ps(self) -> None:\n        \"\"\"Substring match: 'ECOG PS' is an alias.\"\"\"\n        result = normalize_ordinal_value(\"3\", \"ECOG PS\")\n        assert result is not None\n        _, unit_cid = result\n        assert unit_cid == 8527\n\n    def test_substring_entity_contains_alias(self) -> None:\n        \"\"\"Entity text containing an alias: 'Patient ECOG status'.\"\"\"\n        result = normalize_ordinal_value(\"1\", \"Patient ECOG status\")\n        assert result is not None\n        _, unit_cid = result\n        assert unit_cid == 8527\n\n    # --- ECOG grade recognition ---\n\n    def test_ecog_grade_0(self) -> None:\n        \"\"\"ECOG grade 0 returns unit_concept_id=8527.\"\"\"\n        result = normalize_ordinal_value(\"0\", \"ECOG\")\n        assert result is not None\n        _, unit_cid = result\n        assert unit_cid == 8527\n\n    def test_ecog_grade_5(self) -> None:\n        \"\"\"ECOG grade 5 returns unit_concept_id=8527.\"\"\"\n        result = normalize_ordinal_value(\"5\", \"ECOG\")\n        assert result is not None\n        _, unit_cid = result\n        assert unit_cid == 8527\n\n    def test_ecog_all_grades_recognized(self) -> None:\n        \"\"\"All 6 ECOG grades (0-5) are recognized.\"\"\"\n        for grade in range(6):\n            result = normalize_ordinal_value(str(grade), \"ECOG\")\n            assert result is not None, f\"ECOG grade {grade} not recognized\"\n            _, unit_cid = result\n            assert unit_cid == 8527\n\n    # --- Value parsing edge cases ---\n\n    def test_float_normalization(self) -> None:\n        \"\"\"'2.0' normalizes to '2' for grade lookup.\"\"\"\n        result = normalize_ordinal_value(\"2.0\", \"ECOG\")\n        assert result is not None\n        _, unit_cid = result\n        assert unit_cid == 8527\n\n    def test_grade_prefix_strip(self) -> None:\n        \"\"\"'Grade 2' strips prefix and matches grade 2.\"\"\"\n        result = normalize_ordinal_value(\"Grade 2\", \"ECOG\")\n        assert result is not None\n        _, unit_cid = result\n        assert unit_cid == 8527\n\n    def test_class_prefix_strip(self) -> None:\n        \"\"\"'Class 3' strips prefix for NYHA.\"\"\"\n        result = normalize_ordinal_value(\"Class 3\", \"NYHA\")\n        assert result is not None\n        _, unit_cid = result\n        assert unit_cid == 8527\n\n    def test_unknown_grade(self) -> None:\n        \"\"\"Unknown ECOG grade 7 returns (None, 8527).\"\"\"\n        result = normalize_ordinal_value(\"7\", \"ECOG\")\n        assert result is not None\n        value_cid, unit_cid = result\n        assert value_cid is None\n        assert unit_cid == 8527\n\n    # --- Non-ordinal entities ---\n\n    def test_non_ordinal_entity(self) -> None:\n        \"\"\"Non-ordinal entity 'HbA1c' returns None (not a tuple).\"\"\"\n        result = normalize_ordinal_value(\"6.5\", \"HbA1c\")\n        assert result is None\n\n    def test_none_entity(self) -> None:\n        \"\"\"None entity returns None.\"\"\"\n        result = normalize_ordinal_value(\"2\", None)\n        assert result is None\n\n    def test_empty_entity(self) -> None:\n        \"\"\"Empty entity returns None.\"\"\"\n        result = normalize_ordinal_value(\"2\", \"\")\n        assert result is None\n\n    # --- Karnofsky ---\n\n    def test_karnofsky_match(self) -> None:\n        \"\"\"Karnofsky scale recognized with unit_concept_id=8527.\"\"\"\n        result = normalize_ordinal_value(\"80\", \"KPS\")\n        assert result is not None\n        _, unit_cid = result\n        assert unit_cid == 8527\n\n    # --- {score} unit via normalize_unit ---\n\n    def test_score_unit(self) -> None:\n        \"\"\"'score' alias resolves to {score} / 8527.\"\"\"\n        ucum, omop_id = normalize_unit(\"score\")\n        assert ucum == \"{score}\"\n        assert omop_id == 8527\n\n    # --- propose_ordinal_mappings ---\n\n    def test_propose_returns_missing(self) -> None:\n        \"\"\"propose_ordinal_mappings() returns entries without omop_value_concept_id.\"\"\"\n        missing = propose_ordinal_mappings()\n        assert isinstance(missing, list)\n        assert len(missing) > 0\n        # All should have status 'needs_resolution'\n        for entry in missing:\n            assert entry[\"status\"] == \"needs_resolution\"\n            assert \"scale\" in entry\n            assert \"grade\" in entry"
}
```

All done. Here's a summary of what was implemented:

## Phase 3b: Ordinal Scale Normalization — Complete

### Changes Made

**1. `config/ucum_mappings.yaml`** — Added `{score}` unit (OMOP 8527) and full `ordinal_scales` section with ECOG (6 grades), Karnofsky (11 grades), and NYHA (4 classes). Each scale has entity aliases, LOINC codes, SNOMED codes, and structural slots for future `omop_value_concept_id` resolution.

**2. `tools/unit_normalizer.py`** — Added three new functions:
- `_load_ordinal_scales()` — cached YAML loader for ordinal scale definitions
- `normalize_ordinal_value(value_text, entity_text)` — entity-context-aware ordinal normalization with alias matching (exact, bidirectional substring), prefix stripping ("Grade 2" → "2"), and float normalization ("2.0" → "2"). Returns `(value_concept_id, unit_concept_id=8527)` for ordinal entities, `None` for non-ordinal.
- `propose_ordinal_mappings()` — read-only utility that identifies YAML entries missing `omop_value_concept_id` for agent/approval workflow

**3. `tools/structure_builder.py`** — Added ordinal-first dispatch in `_create_atomic_from_mapping()`: checks entity against ordinal scales before falling through to categorical value normalization.

**4. `tests/test_unit_normalizer.py`** — Added `TestNormalizeOrdinalValue` class with 18 tests covering entity matching, grade recognition, edge cases, non-ordinal fallthrough, Karnofsky, `{score}` unit, and `propose_ordinal_mappings()`.

**5. `tests/test_phase3_integration.py`** — Updated ECOG expectations (`unit_concept_id`: None → 8527), added Karnofsky criterion, updated coverage counts.

### Results
- **175 tests pass** (48 unit normalizer, 3 integration, 124 others)
- **ruff check**: clean
- ECOG/Karnofsky criteria now get `unit_concept_id=8527` (`{score}`)
- `value_concept_id` stays `None` until OMOP IDs are resolved via agent workflow and added to YAML

## Protocol Processor Service - E2E Backend Overview

I've completed a thorough exploration of the protocol-processor-service. Here's a comprehensive guide for running and testing it end-to-end:

---

### 1. MAIN ENTRY POINT / SERVER FILE

**File:** `/Users/noahdolevelixir/Code/medgemma-hackathon/services/protocol-processor-service/src/protocol_processor/main.py`

```python
from protocol_processor.graph import create_graph, get_graph
```

The service exports:
- `create_graph(checkpointer=None)` - Creates and compiles the 6-node LangGraph workflow
- `get_graph()` - Async function that returns a singleton graph with optional PostgreSQL checkpointing

The graph is NOT a FastAPI server itself. Instead, it's a **LangGraph StateGraph** that:
- Gets invoked by the api-service via an outbox event handler
- Is triggered by `ProtocolUploaded` events from the event stream
- Runs the full pipeline via `handle_protocol_uploaded()` in trigger.py

---

### 2. E2E TEST SCRIPTS & INTEGRATION TEST PATTERNS

**Main E2E Test Files:**

1. **`tests/test_graph.py`** - Graph compilation and routing tests
   - Tests the 6-node graph compiles correctly
   - Tests should_continue routing logic
   - No live APIs or DB required

2. **`tests/test_phase2_e2e.py`** - Full integration with real SQLite DB
   - Tests database schema (atomic_criteria, composite_criteria, criterion_relationships)
   - Tests ORM CRUD operations (SQLModel)
   - Tests structure builder with real DB writes
   - Tests structure node pipeline integration
   - Tests phase 3 unit/value normalization

3. **`tests/test_phase3_integration.py`** - Realistic clinical criteria
   - 13 realistic clinical trial criteria (HbA1c, Age, eGFR, BMI, Blood Pressure, etc.)
   - Tests unit concept ID resolution (%, years, mL/min, kg/m2, mmHg, etc.)
   - Tests value concept ID resolution (positive, negative)
   - Tests inclusion/exclusion tagging

**To run E2E tests:**
```bash
# All E2E tests
uv run pytest tests/test_phase2_e2e.py -v

# Phase 3 integration tests
uv run pytest tests/test_phase3_integration.py -v

# Graph structure tests
uv run pytest tests/test_graph.py -v
```

### 3. GRAPH/PIPELINE STRUCTURE (LangGraph Data Flow)

**File:** `/Users/noahdolevelixir/Code/medgemma-hackathon/services/protocol-processor-service/src/protocol_processor/graph.py`

**Pipeline Flow:**
```
START → ingest → extract → parse → ground → persist → structure → END
```

**6 Nodes:**

1. **`ingest`** (`nodes/ingest.py`)
   - Fetches PDF bytes from GCS or local storage
   - Updates protocol status to "extracting"
   - Populates state: `pdf_bytes`

2. **`extract`** (`nodes/extract.py`)
   - Calls Gemini API with structured output for criteria extraction
   - Returns JSON string of extracted criteria
   - Populates state: `extraction_json`

3. **`parse`** (`nodes/parse.py`)
   - Parses extraction_json into ExtractionResult
   - Creates CriteriaBatch and Criteria DB records
   - Builds entities_json for grounding
   - Clears pdf_bytes from state (size optimization)
   - Populates state: `batch_id`, `entities_json`

4. **`ground`** (`nodes/ground.py`)
   - Routes entities via TerminologyRouter (UMLS/SNOMED)
   - Selects best match with MedGemma LLM
   - Generates field_mappings per entity
   - Error accumulation: individual entity failures don't block others
   - Populates state: `grounded_entities_json`, error accumulation

5. **`persist`** (`nodes/persist.py`)
   - Commits grounding results to DB
   - Stores field_mappings in Criteria.conditions JSONB
   - Updates protocol status to "pending_review"
   - Handles partial success vs total failure

6. **`structure`** (`nodes/structure.py`)
   - Builds expression trees from persisted criteria
   - Detects AND/OR/NOT logic via Gemini
   - Creates normalized atomic_criteria/composite_criteria records
   - Stores expression tree as structured_criterion JSONB
   - Populates: atomic_criteria, composite_criteria, criterion_relationships tables

**Error Routing:**
- After ingest, extract, parse: conditional edges route to END on fatal error
- After ground, persist, structure: error accumulation (failures logged but continue)

**State Type:**
```python
class PipelineState(TypedDict):
    protocol_id: str
    file_uri: str
    title: str
    batch_id: str | None
    pdf_bytes: bytes | None
    extraction_json: str | None
    entities_json: str | None
    grounded_entities_json: str | None
    status: Literal["processing", "completed", "failed"]
    error: str | None
    errors: list[str]  # Accumulated non-fatal errors
```

---

### 4. HOW TO INVOKE THE PIPELINE PROGRAMMATICALLY

**File:** `/Users/noahdolevelixir/Code/medgemma-hackathon/services/protocol-processor-service/src/protocol_processor/trigger.py`

**Method 1: Via Outbox Event Handler (Production)**
```python
from protocol_processor.trigger import handle_protocol_uploaded

payload = {
    "protocol_id": "proto-123",
    "file_uri": "local://path/to/protocol.pdf",  # gs:// or local://
    "title": "Study Protocol for Diabetes Trial",
    "archived_reviewed_criteria": None  # Optional for re-extraction
}

handle_protocol_uploaded(payload)  # Synchronous, uses asyncio.run internally
```

**Method 2: Direct Graph Invocation (Testing)**
```python
import asyncio
from protocol_processor.graph import get_graph

async def run_pipeline():
    graph = await get_graph()
    
    initial_state = {
        "protocol_id": "test-proto-123",
        "file_uri": "local://test.pdf",
        "title": "Test Protocol",
        "batch_id": None,
        "pdf_bytes": None,
        "extraction_json": None,
        "entities_json": None,
        "grounded_entities_json": None,
        "status": "processing",
        "error": None,
        "errors": [],
    }
    
    config = {"configurable": {"thread_id": f"test-proto-123:unique-uuid"}}
    
    result = await graph.ainvoke(initial_state, config)
    return result

asyncio.run(run_pipeline())
```

**Method 3: Checkpoint & Retry**
```python
from protocol_processor.trigger import retry_from_checkpoint

# Resume from last checkpoint
result = asyncio.run(retry_from_checkpoint("proto-123"))
```

---

### 6. KEY ARCHITECTURAL PATTERNS

**State as JSON Strings (Not Objects):**
- Complex data stored as JSON strings in state to minimize LangGraph serialization overhead
- extraction_json, entities_json, grounded_entities_json are all string-serialized

**Error Accumulation vs Fatal Errors:**
- `state.error` (singular) = fatal error → routes to END
- `state.errors` (plural list) = accumulated non-fatal errors → continues processing

**Database Access from Nodes:**
- Nodes ARE allowed to import from api-service for DB access
- Each node can read/write to the shared PostgreSQL database via SQLModel

**Checkpointing:**
- Optional PostgreSQL checkpoint storage for fault tolerance
- Each invocation gets unique thread_id: `f"{protocol_id}:{uuid4()}"`
- Stored in protocol.metadata_["pipeline_thread_id"] for retry_from_checkpoint

**MLflow Tracing:**
- Each node creates separate MLflow trace inside the async context
- Traces appear in MLflow UI tagged with protocol_id for filtering

kps_entries)\n\n\nclass TestNormalizeOrdinalDirectE2E:\n    \"\"\"Direct normalize_ordinal_value() tests with various real-world inputs.\"\"\"\n\n    def test_who_performance_status_alias(self) -> None:\n        \"\"\"'WHO performance status' is an ECOG alias.\"\"\"\n        result = normalize_ordinal_value(\"1\", \"WHO performance status\")\n        assert result is not None\n        _, unit_cid = result\n        assert unit_cid == 8527\n\n    def test_zubrod_score_alias(self) -> None:\n        \"\"\"'Zubrod score' is an ECOG alias.\"\"\"\n        result = normalize_ordinal_value(\"0\", \"Zubrod score\")\n        assert result is not None\n        _, unit_cid = result\n        assert unit_cid == 8527\n\n    def test_eastern_cooperative_oncology_group(self) -> None:\n        \"\"\"Full formal name matches.\"\"\"\n        result = normalize_ordinal_value(\"2\", \"Eastern Cooperative Oncology Group\")\n        assert result is not None\n        _, unit_cid = result\n        assert unit_cid == 8527\n\n    def test_new_york_heart_association(self) -> None:\n        \"\"\"Full NYHA name matches.\"\"\"\n        result = normalize_ordinal_value(\"1\", \"New York Heart Association\")\n        assert result is not None\n        _, unit_cid = result\n        assert unit_cid == 8527\n\n    def test_karnofsky_performance_scale(self) -> None:\n        \"\"\"Alternative Karnofsky alias.\"\"\"\n        result = normalize_ordinal_value(\"90\", \"Karnofsky performance scale\")\n        assert result is not None\n        _, unit_cid = result\n        assert unit_cid == 8527\n\n    def test_empty_value_still_identifies_scale(self) -> None:\n        \"\"\"Empty value with ordinal entity still returns unit_concept_id.\"\"\"\n        result = normalize_ordinal_value(\"\", \"ECOG\")\n        assert result is not None\n        value_cid, unit_cid = result\n        assert value_cid is None\n        assert unit_cid == 8527\n\n    def test_none_value_still_identifies_scale(self) -> None:\n        \"\"\"None value with ordinal entity still returns unit_concept_id.\"\"\"\n        result = normalize_ordinal_value(None, \"ECOG\")\n        assert result is not None\n        value_cid, unit_cid = result\n        assert value_cid is None\n        assert unit_cid == 8527\n\n    def test_score_prefix_strip(self) -> None:\n        \"\"\"'Score 3' strips prefix for ECOG.\"\"\"\n        result = normalize_ordinal_value(\"Score 3\", \"ECOG\")\n        assert result is not None\n        _, unit_cid = result\n        assert unit_cid == 8527\n\n    def test_level_prefix_strip(self) -> None:\n        \"\"\"'Level 2' strips prefix.\"\"\"\n        result = normalize_ordinal_value(\"Level 2\", \"NYHA\")\n        assert result is not None\n        _, unit_cid = result\n        assert unit_cid == 8527\n"
```json
}
```

### `TestPhase3bOrdinalE2E` (4 tests) — Full backend path through `build_expression_tree` → DB
- **8 criteria** (ECOG, ECOG PS, Karnofsky, KPS, NYHA functional class, NYHA class, HbA1c, HIV) all produce correct `unit_concept_id` in the DB
- Ordinal entities → `8527` ({score}), lab values → `8554` (%), categorical → `None`
- **"Grade 2"** prefix stripping works end-to-end through the backend
- **"2.0"** float normalization works through the backend (`value_numeric=2.0`, `unit_concept_id=8527`)
- **Mixed batch**: ordinal + lab + ordinal in the same batch — each gets its correct unit

### `TestProposeOrdinalMappingsE2E` (4 tests) — Agent utility
- All 3 scales (ECOG, Karnofsky, NYHA) appear in proposals
- All 6 ECOG grades listed for resolution
- SNOMED codes present where expected (ECOG: yes, Karnofsky: no)

### `TestNormalizeOrdinalDirectE2E` (9 tests) — Real-world alias coverage
- "WHO performance status", "Zubrod score", "Eastern Cooperative Oncology Group" → ECOG
- "New York Heart Association" → NYHA
- "Karnofsky performance scale" → Karnofsky
- Empty/None value still identifies the scale and returns `unit_concept_id=8527`
- "Score 3", "Level 2" prefix stripping works

## Testing Patterns and Configuration Analysis for Protocol Processor Service

I've thoroughly explored the testing patterns and configuration for the protocol-processor-service. Here's a comprehensive summary:

### 1. **Test Structure Overview**

**Files explored:**
- `/tests/test_graph.py` - Graph compilation and routing tests
- `/tests/test_phase2_structure.py` - Structure node testing with mocking and DB setup
- `/tests/conftest.py` - Shared test configuration
- `/pyproject.toml` - Dependencies and test configuration
- `/src/protocol_processor/tools/structure_builder.py` - LLM structured output implementation
- `/src/protocol_processor/nodes/structure.py` - Structure node pipeline implementation
- `/src/protocol_processor/schemas/structure.py` - Data models

---

### 2. **How LLM Calls Are Mocked in Tests**

The test suite uses **LangChain's structured output pattern** with `langchain_google_genai.ChatGoogleGenerativeAI`. Mocking approach:

```python
# Pattern from test_phase2_structure.py (lines 303-336)
@patch.dict(os.environ, {"GOOGLE_API_KEY": "test-key"})
@patch("langchain_google_genai.ChatGoogleGenerativeAI")
async def test_successful_and_detection(self, mock_chat_cls: MagicMock) -> None:
    # 1. Create the expected Pydantic response object
```

### 4. **How Nodes with DB Access Get Their Sessions**

**Pattern from structure.py (lines 115-172):**

```python
async def structure_node(state: PipelineState) -> dict[str, Any]:
    # Create a session from engine
    with Session(engine) as session:
        # Query criteria
        stmt = select(Criteria).where(Criteria.batch_id == batch_id)
        all_criteria = session.exec(stmt).all()
        
        # Pass session to async processors
        semaphore = asyncio.Semaphore(4)
        tasks = [
            _process_criterion(c, protocol_id, session, semaphore)
            for c in qualifying
        ]
        results = await asyncio.gather(*tasks)
        session.commit()
```
